- **q-c511c52d1826a3** `community_question_bank` — TTFT (time to first token)**: request arrival → first generated token. Driven by queue wait + prefill time, so it scales with prompt length and system load. This is the &quot;is it responsive?&quot; metric. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/08-inference-and-production/questions.md#L76)
- **q-d5ee3c159cea49** `community_question_bank` — TPOT (time per output token)**, a.k.a. inter-token latency (ITL): average gap between subsequent tokens during decode. Driven by memory bandwidth, model size, batch contention, and interference from other requests&#x27; prefills. This is the &quot;does the stream feel smooth?&quot; metric. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/08-inference-and-production/questions.md#L77)

- **q-bb424016fef441** `community_question_bank` — The **query rewrite step is what makes multi-turn RAG work**: &quot;does that apply to interns?&quot; retrieves nothing; the rewritten &quot;does the remote-work policy apply to interns?&quot; does. - **q-5b244537389834** `community_question_bank` — What&#x27;s the enforcement latency requirement?** Pre-publish blocking (content held until cleared) vs post-publish takedown (visible immediately, removed if flagged)? → *Assume:* post-publish for most content with a p99 of 30s to first automated decision; pre-publish only for new/low-trust accounts and known-risky surfaces (DMs to minors, livestream titles). [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L13)
- **q-f7721462e54b6f** `community_question_bank` — What are the policy classes and their severity tiers?** → *Assume:* ~15 policy classes; a small &quot;zero-tolerance&quot; set (CSAM, credible threats) where recall is paramount, and a large &quot;contextual&quot; set (harassment, misinformation) where precision matters more. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L14)
- **q-87f17c3e51dab6** `community_question_bank` — What&#x27;s the language mix?** → *Assume:* 40% English, long tail of 50+ languages; top 10 languages cover 90% of volume. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L15)
- **q-715441e7aed4ab** `community_question_bank` — What human review capacity exists?** → *Assume:* budget for reviewing ~0.4% of daily volume (~40-50k items/day), so automation must resolve 99.6%. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L16)
- **q-576d903a050eb0** `community_question_bank` — Are there legal/regulatory constraints?** → *Assume:* DSA-style transparency obligations (statement of reasons for each removal, appeal rights), and mandatory reporting for CSAM (hash-match against industry databases, never store the content). [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L17)
- **q-08e8b57e63d090** `community_question_bank` — What does &quot;an item&quot; look like?** → *Assume:* median text item ~40 tokens (a comment); posts up to ~2k tokens; images handled by a parallel visual pipeline (hashing + vision classifier), converging at the same decision layer. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L18)
- **q-200a0e306e0f1c** `community_question_bank` — Suppose harassment tier-1 scores, at `t_block = 0.95`, give precision 0.92 / recall 0.60. Lowering to 0.90 raises recall to 0.68 but drops precision to 0.85. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L104)
- **q-30c208e572358e** `community_question_bank` — Your recall target for hate speech and your precision target conflict. Who decides the operating point? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L245)
- **q-3c9bea3ec7bbbd** `community_question_bank` — How do you moderate livestreams / real-time chat? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L246)
- **q-64a245b7f41141** `community_question_bank` — Why not fine-tune one big multitask model instead of the tier stack? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L248)
- **q-108fc8f93a0f9e** `community_question_bank` — How would you handle a brand-new policy class launching next week? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L249)
- **q-d666f23a4628a3** `community_question_bank` — What if the platform adds end-to-end encrypted DMs? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/05-content-moderation-pipeline.md#L250)
- **q-cc2a96fdc91291** `community_question_bank` — Latency: batch or interactive?** → *Assume:* 95% batch (nightly/hourly ERP sync; SLA &quot;done by 8am&quot;), 5% interactive uploads where a user watches (p95 &lt; 30s/doc). This split drives the whole cost design. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L11)
- **q-c48909a3426892** `community_question_bank` — Document mix and quality?** → *Assume:* 250k docs/day: ~240k invoices/receipts (1-3 pages, ~30% scans/photos of varying quality, 70% digital PDFs) and ~10k contracts (10-100 pages, mostly digital). [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L12)
- **q-12b6fb45b58a11** `community_question_bank` — Schema: fixed or customer-defined?** → *Assume:* core schemas fixed per doc type + per-customer custom fields (e.g., cost-centre codes) - so the extraction layer must be schema-driven, not hardcoded. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L13)
- **q-fd7826f65b4000** `community_question_bank` — Accuracy bar?** → *Assume:* ≥ 99.5% field-level accuracy on *money fields* of what we auto-approve (errors here are financial loss); softer targets on descriptive fields. Human review budget ~8-10% of documents. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L14)
- **q-df9bf63ffe5b05** `community_question_bank` — Language/geography?** → *Assume:* invoices in 20+ languages/locales - date formats (`03/04/2026`), decimal conventions (`1.234,56 €`), tax regimes (VAT vs sales tax) all vary. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L15)
- **q-6d307a20aa4ddc** `community_question_bank` — Compliance?** → *Assume:* SOC 2, customer data isolation, some customers forbid data leaving region → need region-pinned processing and a self-hosted fallback path for the strictest tenants. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L16)
- **q-eb4561f3a91bc6** `community_question_bank` — What never goes in a prompt:** other tenants&#x27; documents as few-shot examples. Few-shots come from synthetic documents or licensed/public samples; it&#x27;s both a contractual and a leak-surface issue, and interviewers increasingly probe for it. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L181)
- **q-07b8cfbab5fde6** `community_question_bank` — Why not fine-tune a traditional doc-AI model (LayoutLM-style) instead of prompting a VLM? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L244)
- **q-5602d2d3d87e85** `community_question_bank` — How do you handle a 500-page contract with amendments that override earlier clauses? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L246)
- **q-82317a664a0463** `community_question_bank` — When does self-hosting beat the API here? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L248)
- **q-165d945eb09abe** `community_question_bank` — An attacker submits invoices designed to be misread - inflated totals in a layout your model fumbles. How do you even detect this? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L249)
- **q-2523fe28f7e374** `community_question_bank` — How would you onboard a brand-new document type - say, customs forms - in a week? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/06-document-intelligence-pipeline.md#L250)
- **q-9ea563dfd86e93** `community_question_bank` — Who are the users and what&#x27;s the tolerance for error?** → *Assume:* ~5,000 employees; mixed SQL-literacy. Answers inform decisions, so correctness &gt; coverage: the system should refuse/clarify rather than guess. Analysts will inspect SQL; execs won&#x27;t. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L11)
- **q-419530dfc761bb** `community_question_bank` — Warehouse shape?** → *Assume:* ~10,000 tables / 150,000 columns across raw, staging, and curated layers; ~300 well-modelled curated tables (dbt marts) cover ~80% of real questions; documentation quality is uneven. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L12)
- **q-b50864b99e3acb** `community_question_bank` — Is there a semantic layer?** → *Assume:* partial - some certified metric definitions (dbt metrics / LookML-class) exist for ~50 core metrics. This matters enormously; a candidate who asks this question signals experience. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L13)
- **q-b07bcf5e9e23d7** `community_question_bank` — Latency expectations?** → *Assume:* p50 ≤ 15s, p95 ≤ 60s end-to-end including warehouse execution - chat-analytics, not dashboards. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L14)
- **q-5b91664fcad636** `community_question_bank` — Write access?** → *Never.* Read-only by construction, plus per-user row/column-level security must hold (the agent must not become a permission bypass). [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L15)
- **q-c92a104bcf00ec** `community_question_bank` — Volume?** → *Assume:* 20k questions/day steady state, 3× at quarter-end. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L16)
- **q-9c624cf910830c** `community_question_bank` — Ambiguity detection → clarifying question (&quot;Which revenue: bookings, GAAP, or ARR?&quot;) instead of silent assumption; state assumptions inline when they&#x27;re minor. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L23)
- **q-e9709ab07f76f5** `community_question_bank` — Your execution accuracy is 85%. Is that shippable? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L245)
- **q-0e9dd4f3ce2e04** `community_question_bank` — How would you use multi-step agentic execution instead of one-shot generation? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L246)
- **q-6419a7ef1de1b5** `community_question_bank` — Fine-tune a smaller model on your query log? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L247)

- **q-370cd2846e0275** `community_question_bank` — How do you keep this from becoming a DDoS on your data team&#x27;s trust? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L249)
- **q-9825866341f240** `community_question_bank` — A user asks a question whose correct answer requires a table they lack access to. What should happen? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/07-text-to-sql-agent.md#L250)
- **q-eb94c54073bbf2** `community_question_bank` — Capture mechanism?** Bot participant that joins the call vs platform-native media APIs vs desktop client audio? → *Assume:* bot participant as the baseline (works everywhere, visibly announces itself - which is also a consent feature), platform APIs where available. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L11)
- **q-acf6436c458755** `community_question_bank` — Live features or post-hoc only?** → *Assume:* both, but asymmetric: live transcript + lightweight rolling notes during the call; the *authoritative* summary and action items are computed post-hoc where we can afford better models and full context. ~30% of meetings have anyone looking at the live view. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L12)
- **q-ee75d7ca07aee5** `community_question_bank` — Scale?** → *Assume:* 50k meetings/day, avg 45 min, avg 5 participants; peak concurrency ~4,000 simultaneous meetings (mid-morning across time zones). [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L13)
- **q-47aa0ddf0c0698** `community_question_bank` — Languages/accents?** → *Assume:* 80% English (global accents), top-8 other languages for the rest; code-switching happens. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L14)
- **q-99e4415c410e1c** `community_question_bank` — Consent/regulatory posture?** → *Assume:* enterprise customers in the US + EU: two-party-consent jurisdictions exist, GDPR applies, some tenants demand EU-only processing and zero training on their data. Consent UX is a product requirement, not an afterthought. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L15)
- **q-7db44d3df11c9a** `community_question_bank` — Accuracy bar for action items?** → *Assume:* precision matters more than recall - a missed action item is annoying; an invented one, attributed to a named person and synced to Jira, is trust-destroying. Target: hallucinated action-item rate ≈ 0 on eval, &lt; 2% observed. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L16)
- **q-335ce36d98858e** `community_question_bank` — Why not do everything live and skip the post-hoc pass? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L245)

- **q-0ab9261aa52402** `community_question_bank` — What&#x27;s your single most important metric for the first launch? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L249)
- **q-2b9acd788504d9** `community_question_bank` — Where does the next 10× cost reduction come from? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L250)
- **q-2dbb6037d3ee11** `community_question_bank` — Search across all my past meetings - what changes architecturally? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/08-meeting-assistant.md#L251)
- **q-f476f447064c1c** `community_question_bank` — Endpointing is the single largest line item and it is not an engineering constant, it is a product decision.** A 200 ms trailing-silence threshold interrupts anyone who pauses to think or reads a phone number in chunks. A 700 ms threshold makes the agent feel sluggish on every short answer. So make it stateful: tight (roughly 150-250 ms) when the expected answer is short and constrained (&quot;how many people?&quot;), loose (roughly 500-800 ms) when the caller is likely mid-list or reading digits, and let a lightweight semantic check on the partial transcript hold the turn open when the text is obviously incomplete (&quot;my number is four one five&quot;). [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/09-realtime-voice-agent.md#L119)
- **q-64030c1eb2c078** `community_question_bank` — Narrow first, then offer two.** Reading six available times aloud is unusable - callers retain the first and last. The strategy is to constrain, then present at most two options: &quot;Friday at 7 is full. I have 6:30 or 8:15, which works better?&quot; This is a dialogue-design change, not a prompt tweak, and it is the thing most chat-to-voice ports get wrong. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/09-realtime-voice-agent.md#L182)
- **q-805116c909aabd** `community_question_bank` — WER-conditioned test cases.** Replay real dialogues with realistic ASR errors injected from the observed confusion matrix, not random noise, and assert the dialogue policy recovers: does it re-ask, does it confirm, or does it silently book the wrong day? This is the suite that catches agents which look great in text and fail on the phone. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/09-realtime-voice-agent.md#L248)
- **q-9f05f52c4907c6** `community_question_bank` — Caller-experience signals: hang-up within the first 10 seconds (the &quot;I want a human&quot; tell), barge-in rate, repeat-request rate (&quot;sorry, what?&quot; from either side), and post-call SMS satisfaction. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/11-ai-system-design/case-studies/09-realtime-voice-agent.md#L268)

- **q-ca8aa679aeaeee** `community_question_bank` — &quot;On-device or cloud?&quot; became the signature mobile-AI system-design question.** Interviewers hand you a feature (&quot;summarize the user&#x27;s messages&quot;, &quot;camera plant identifier&quot;) and grade how you reason across latency, privacy, cost, offline, quality ceiling, and fleet coverage. Candidates who default to &quot;call the OpenAI API&quot; without touching the on-device option get recalibrated fast - the platforms shipped real alternatives and interviewers expect you to know them. - **q-c70383a53f06df** `community_question_bank` — &quot;How would you test this?&quot; now has a non-deterministic subject.** The classic design-a-test-plan round is still the spine of the loop, but the feature under test is a support chatbot, a summariser, or an agent - and interviewers immediately probe whether you reach for exact-match assertions (fail) or graded evals, property checks, and statistical thresholds (pass). - **q-fa286995215ce5** `community_question_bank` — Check temperature** — is temperature &gt; 0? Non-determinism is by design. For factual consistency, set temperature = 0. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/topics/07-ai-system-design.md#L296)
- **q-4fe01d27cfe5bb** `community_question_bank` — Check context variability** — is the conversation history included? Different history lengths alter the model&#x27;s context and therefore response. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/topics/07-ai-system-design.md#L297)
- **q-e0a2e595d42473** `community_question_bank` — Check retrieval variability** — if using RAG, are different chunks being retrieved each time? Retrieval non-determinism propagates to generation. Fix: ensure deterministic chunking, stable vector index. - **q-d28cbab7fd046a** `community_question_bank` — Can you reason about *your own* system under failure?** The &quot;5pp eval regression&quot; question rewards a debugging method, not a lucky guess. - **q-4ba48e31fd212b** `community_question_bank` — &quot;Temperature &amp; top-p?&quot;** → temperature rescales logits before softmax; top-p keeps a cumulative-mass nucleus; tune one, not both. **temperature 0 is greedy, not deterministic** — design for variation with schemas + eval gates. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/01-llm-fundamentals.md#L367)
- **q-9162b606be8b1c** `community_question_bank` — &quot;Reasoning model vs standard?&quot;** → hard multi-step / verification-heavy → reasoning; latency/cost/volume-sensitive and shallow → standard; route per request rather than choosing globally. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/01-llm-fundamentals.md#L369)
- **q-91fde6e6fea185** `community_question_bank` — &quot;Is RAG still relevant with long context?&quot;** → Yes — RAG is retrieval + grounding + freshness + ACLs. Long context loses on cost, latency (prefill), recall (lost-in-the-middle), and attribution. It&#x27;s a complement for single-document holistic tasks, not a corpus replacement. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/02-rag.md#L269)
- **q-7b47b382e668ad** `community_question_bank` — How to open — clarify before you draw a single box [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L40)
- **q-90836f800a5212** `community_question_bank` — Latency SLO** — convert &quot;how fast?&quot; into a millisecond budget *before* picking a model. For LLM chat, split it: TTFT vs total. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L48)
- **q-b4d3e1347bf357** `community_question_bank` — Data &amp; eval set.** Golden set of historically-labeled tickets (category, priority, resolution). The end-state is what matters, so evaluate **trace-based**: did it pick the right category, call the right tools in the right order, and reach the right terminal state? Metric: routing accuracy, priority F1, auto-resolution rate; counter-metric: **mis-escalation / false-auto-resolve** rate. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L166)
- **q-c63d7c8b1d8ccc** `community_question_bank` — Design ChatGPT / a Claude chat service.** Frame as interactive LLM serving. Lead with the two-number latency SLO (TTFT vs ITL), continuous batching + PagedAttention for throughput, prefix caching for the system prompt, KV-cache math, and autoscale-on-queue-depth with a min-replica buffer. Streaming makes perceived latency ≪ total. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L185)
- **q-70c9b4e328fdc1** `community_question_bank` — Design a RAG system.** ingest → chunk → embed → **hybrid** retrieve → **rerank** → generate-with-citations → eval. Lead **evaluation-first** (RAGAS + LLM-as-judge, no ground truth). Hybrid beats pure-vector 15–30%; the reranker is the highest-ROI component. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L186)
- **q-4f509a06ab02f8** `community_question_bank` — Design LLM inference at scale.** See worked design 2 — quantization (FP8), continuous batching (8–23×), KV cache + PagedAttention, prefix caching, autoscale on queue depth, cold-start buffer. Give a $/1k-token number. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L187)
- **q-8ebb18725b85d7** `community_question_bank` — Design an agent (ticket triage / research).** Planner/executor, function calling, memory with decay, **trace-based eval**, confidence-gated human-in-the-loop, tool-call validation, observability. Cascade the easy tail to a cheap model. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L188)
- **q-ae8211ab741181** `community_question_bank` — Hallucination-free banking chatbot.** There&#x27;s no &quot;hallucination-free&quot; — reframe as *bounded* hallucination. Mandatory citations (every claim traces to a source), a refusal path for anything not grounded, faithfulness measured separately from retrieval recall, strict PII/compliance guardrails + audit log, and a human-escalation threshold. Answer &quot;can we ship at 5%?&quot; in *business* terms. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L189)
- **q-a51af412c4c1f5** `community_question_bank` — &quot;How would you open?&quot;** Clarify target `Y`, scale, latency SLO, quality bar — *then* draw. Say the numbers you&#x27;re assuming and confirm them. [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L191)
- **q-80ed78ed968a95** `community_question_bank` — &quot;How do you handle a 10× / Black-Friday spike?&quot;** Min-replica buffer pre-warmed to peak, queue-depth autoscaling, shadow any model upgrade, and **degrade gracefully** — serve a cheaper model or a queued response rather than time out. Never &quot;just autoscale it.&quot; [Source / reading](https://github.com/landedjobs/awesome-ai-engineer-interview/blob/7cbbd2d6a9b2e7390c044df345f54318f2a1b865/content/07-ml-and-llm-system-design.md#L192)
